01000: /* =========================
01001:    API: Day
01002: ========================= */
01003: 
01004: function buildPrizeSignature(draw) {
01005:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
01006:   if (!prizes.length) return "";
01007: 
01008:   const parts = prizes
01009:     .map((p) => {
01010:       const pos = Number(p?.position);
01011:       const grupo = Number(p?.grupo);
01012:       const numero = toPrizeDigitsByPosition(
01013:         p?.numero ?? p?.milhar ?? p?.number ?? p?.num ?? p?.valor ?? "",
01014:         pos
01015:       );
01016: 
01017:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || !Number.isFinite(grupo) || !numero) return "";
01018:       return `${pad2(pos)}:${pad2(grupo)}:${numero}`;
01019:     })
01020:     .filter(Boolean)
01021:     .sort();
01022: 
01023:   return parts.join("|");
01024: }

00270: 
00271:   return { kind: null, bucket: null, hhmm: null };
00272: }
00273: 
00274: function drawPassesHourFilter(draw, hourFilter) {
00275:   if (!hourFilter || !hourFilter.kind) return true;
00276: 
00277:   const raw =
00278:     draw?.close_hour ?? draw?.closeHour ?? draw?.hour ?? draw?.hora ?? "";
00279:   const norm = normalizeHourLike(raw);
00280: 
00281:   if (hourFilter.kind === "exact") {
00282:     return norm === normalizeHourLike(hourFilter.hhmm);
00283:   }
00284: 
00285:   if (hourFilter.kind === "bucket") {
00286:     return toHourBucket(norm) === toHourBucket(hourFilter.bucket);
00287:   }
00288: 
00289:   return true;
00290: }

00425:     };

00436:     }
00437:   }
00438: 
00439:   return order.map((k) => byKey.get(k)).filter(Boolean);

01026: function federalHourPriority(draw) {
01027:   const h = normalizeHourLike(
01028:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
01029:   );
01030:   const ymd = String(draw?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) || "").trim();
01031:   const n = hourToNumSafe(h);
01032: 
01033:   const prefer20h = getFederalPreferredHourForYmd(ymd) === "20:00";
01034: 
01035:   if (prefer20h) {
01036:     if (h === "20:00") return 200000 + n;
01037:     if (h === "19:00") return 190000 + n;
01038:     return 100000 + n;
01039:   }
01040: 
01041:   if (h === "19:00") return 200000 + n;
01042:   if (h === "20:00") return 190000 + n;
01043:   return 100000 + n;
01044: }

01046: function pickBetterFederalClone(a, b) {
01047:   const pa = Number.isFinite(Number(a?.prizesCount))
01048:     ? Number(a.prizesCount)
01049:     : Array.isArray(a?.prizes)
01050:     ? a.prizes.length
01051:     : 0;

01026: function federalHourPriority(draw) {

01046: function pickBetterFederalClone(a, b) {
01047:   const pa = Number.isFinite(Number(a?.prizesCount))
01048:     ? Number(a.prizesCount)
01049:     : Array.isArray(a?.prizes)
01050:     ? a.prizes.length
01051:     : 0;
01052: 
01053:   const pb = Number.isFinite(Number(b?.prizesCount))
01054:     ? Number(b.prizesCount)
01055:     : Array.isArray(b?.prizes)
01056:     ? b.prizes.length
01057:     : 0;
01058: 
01059:   if (pa !== pb) return pb > pa ? b : a;
01060: 
01061:   const ha = federalHourPriority(a);
01062:   const hb = federalHourPriority(b);

01063:   if (ha !== hb) return hb > ha ? b : a;

01070: function collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(draws) {
01071:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01072:   const byKey = new Map();
01073: 
01074:   for (const d of arr) {
01075:     const ymd = String(d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d?.date) || "").trim();
01076:     const sig = buildPrizeSignature(d);
01077: 
01078:     if (!ymd || !sig) {
01079:       const fallbackKey = `id::${String(
01080:         d?.drawId || d?.id || Math.random()
01081:       )}`;
01082:       if (!byKey.has(fallbackKey)) byKey.set(fallbackKey, d);
01083:       continue;
01084:     }
01085: 
01086:     const key = `${ymd}__${sig}`;
01087:     const prev = byKey.get(key);
01088: 
01089:     if (!prev) byKey.set(key, d);
01090:     else byKey.set(key, pickBetterFederalClone(prev, d));
01091:   }
01092: 
01093:   return sortDrawsLocal(Array.from(byKey.values()));
01094: }

01451:       const all = [];
01452:       for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
01453:         const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
01454:         const dayDraws = await getKingResultsByDate({
01455:           uf,
01456:           date: day,
01457:           closeHour,
01458:           closeHourBucket,
01459:           positions: positionsArr,
01460:         });
01461:         if (Array.isArray(dayDraws) && dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
01462:       }
01463: 
01464:       const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
01465:       const base = hourFilter?.kind
01466:         ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01467:         : baseAll;
01468: 
01469:       const ordered = sortDrawsLocal(base);
01470: 
01471:       const conc = chooseRangeConcurrency(ordered.length);
01472:       const results = await mapWithConcurrency(ordered, conc, async (item) => {
01473:         const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
01474:         const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
01475:         return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
01476:       });
01477: 
01478:       const out = dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
01479:       if (!bypassCache) {
01480:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01481:     }
01482:       return out;
01483:     }

----- LINHAS 1567-1609 -----
01567:   return cleaned.length ? Array.from(new Set(cleaned)) : null;
01568: }

01570: function drawPassesLotteriesFilter(draw, lotteriesArr) {
01571:   if (!lotteriesArr || !lotteriesArr.length) return true;
01572:   const code = String(draw?.lottery_code ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
01573:   if (!code) return false;
01574:   return lotteriesArr.includes(code);
01575: }

01577: function lateCacheKey({
01578:   scopeKey,
01579:   fromYmd,
01580:   toYmd,
01581:   baseYmd,
01582:   prizePosition,
01583:   hourFilter,
01584:   lotteriesArr,
01585:   chunkDays,
01586: }) {
01587:   const h =
01588:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
01589:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
01590:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
01591:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
01592:       : "all";
01593:   const lots = lotteriesArr && lotteriesArr.length ? lotteriesArr.join(",") : "all";
01594:   const cd = Number(chunkDays) || 15;
01595:   const pos = Number(prizePosition) || 1;
01596:   return `late::${scopeKey}::${fromYmd}..${toYmd}::base=${baseYmd}::pos=${pos}::h=${h}::lots=${lots}::chunk=${cd}`;
01597: }

01603:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
